    SELECT p0."PurchaseItemId", p0."EventId", p0."LineTotal", p0."PurchaseId", p0."QrCodePath", p0."Quantity", p0."Rating", p0."UnitPrice", e."EventId" AS "EventId0", e."AvailableTickets", e."CategoryId", e."CreatedAt", e."Description", e."EndDateTime", e."ImagePath", e."OrganizerId", e."StartDateTime", e."TicketPrice", e."Title"
    FROM "PurchaseItems" AS p0
    INNER JOIN "Events" AS e ON p0."EventId" = e."EventId"
) AS t0 ON t."PurchaseId" = t0."PurchaseId"
ORDER BY t."PurchaseId", t0."PurchaseItemId"
